| [307](issues/307-her-customers-history-stopped-saying-which-order.md)                                             | minor    | Her customer’s history stopped saying which order                                                        | fixed                             | Checkout composes the order write into its own transaction, so `order.created` was announced before the commit and the number read back as null — four of five orders said "An order was placed" and named none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| [308](issues/308-every-order-she-ever-sent-is-in-nobodys-history.md)                                              | major    | Every order she ever sent is in nobody’s history                                                         | fixed                             | `order.fulfilled` / `order.delivered` carried no `customerId` and the column is nullable, so all six shipped and delivered rows were written against nobody, said nothing, and are reachable from no screen                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| [309](issues/309-she-re-imported-her-list-and-it-put-someone-who-unsubscribed-back-on-it.md)                      | major    | She re-imported her list and it put someone who unsubscribed back on it                                  | fixed                             | 0 new and 25 overwritten reported as "25 of 25 brought over", the same sentence 25 arrivals produce; the overwrite reached the marketing opt-in, so a man who had asked to come off the newsletter was put back on it by a months-old file                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| [310](issues/310-the-people-who-never-confirmed-came-in-as-people-she-can-email.md)                               | major    | The people who never confirmed came in as people she can email                                           | fixed                             | The Mailchimp adapter writes an unconfirmed subscriber as `''` and `row()` drops empty values, so its own "cannot legally mail" rule was deleted a layer down and they landed contactable, in ordinary segments                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| [311](issues/311-she-searched-for-the-screen-by-its-own-name-and-was-told-nothing-matches.md)                     | minor    | She searched for the screen by its own name and was told nothing matches                                 | fixed                             | Past moves was marked unlisted AND given search keywords; the launcher only reads the keywords of surfaces it lists, so its own name returned "Nothing matches that" and the only way in was one button on one other screen                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| [312](issues/312-she-cancelled-the-order-and-took-the-sale-away-from-the-customer-for-good.md)                    | major    | She cancelled the order and took the sale away from the customer for good                                | fixed                             | Nothing anywhere released a discount usage on cancel or refund, so an undone sale spent the customer’s one use for good; and the basket re-checked only the sale’s dates, so the same shopper was refused when she typed the code and served when she did not                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| [313](issues/313-every-design-was-a-release-behind-so-her-customers-could-not-get-back-to-their-account.md)       | major    | Every design was a release behind, so her customers could not get back to their account                  | fixed                             | Not one of the 191 shipped designs carried the live account core — 24 had no account link at all and 167 a stamped “Sign in” — and 47 had no legal links in the footer; the bundles were never regenerated, one navbar variant had no slot to fill, and the heal matched a slot older frames never had                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| [314](issues/314-nothing-told-her-that-her-site-offered-no-way-into-an-account-at-all.md)                         | minor    | Nothing told her that her site offered no way into an account at all                                     | fixed                             | The automatic repair cannot invent a control it never had, so a site with no account link anywhere was silent everywhere; it is a pre-publish check finding, gated on whether the tenant runs a module that gives a customer an account                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| [315](issues/315-it-told-her-to-publish-and-the-publish-button-was-greyed-out.md)                                 | major    | It told her to publish, and the publish button was greyed out                                            | fixed                             | For the owner who has never opened the builder, publishing resolves nothing, so Home's offer led to a disabled button and two contradictory sentences; gaps now carry their source and each gets its own words and remedy                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| [316](issues/316-her-second-site-was-given-an-address-on-another-companys-domain.md)                              | major    | Her second site was given an address on another company's domain                                         | fixed                             | A zone missing from `SPARX_ZONE_DOMAINS` fell through to the sparx default, so a Piggles tenant's second site was minted on `sparx.zone` while the form promised `piggles.site`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| [317](issues/317-the-error-about-her-own-web-address-named-another-companys-domain.md)                            | major    | The error about her own web address named another company's domain                                       | fixed                             | Five refusals on the Web addresses pane named `sparx.zone` to a `piggles.site` tenant; `check:boundaries`' brand rule exempts a brand followed by a dot, which is right 17 times in 18                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| [318](issues/318-four-of-her-sixteen-products-could-not-be-found-by-name.md)                                      | major    | Four of her sixteen products could not be found by name                                                  | fixed                             | The search index was four products short after [281] fixed the pipeline without putting back what was already lost, and the notice that warns about this only fired on a COMPLETELY empty index, so it stayed silent and its Put-them-back button stayed unreachable                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| [319](issues/319-reaching-the-thing-she-had-just-opened-took-62-tab-presses.md)                                   | minor    | Reaching the thing she had just opened took 62 tab presses                                               | fixed                             | The rail (~15 stops) and the pane tab strip (3 per open pane) both precede pane content, and there was no skip control; F6 did nothing either                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| [320](issues/320-the-signups-board-could-not-say-what-kind-of-business-she-runs.md)                               | minor    | The signups board could not say what kind of business she runs                                           | fixed                             | The board read the trade from the console composer's key, which Piggles onboarding never writes — and rewrote a deal's facts only when its NAME changed, so nothing recorded after signup ever reached it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| [321](issues/321-the-box-for-her-own-business-name-suggested-another-companys.md)                                 | copy     | The box for her own business name suggested another company's                                            | fixed                             | `placeholder="WizeWorks"` on the Business name field — the operator's name in the box asking what her business is called                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| [322](issues/322-her-only-buyers-were-at-the-bottom-of-a-list-sorted-by-recent-order.md)                          | major    | Her only buyers were at the bottom of a list sorted by recent order                                      | fixed                             | Postgres sorts `DESC` NULLS FIRST, so everyone who had never ordered came first — and it silently emptied the win-back tool, which took a page of those and then discarded them                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| [323](issues/323-her-customer-page-said-average-order-zero-above-three-orders-worth-502.md)                       | major    | Her customer page said "Average order $0.00" above three orders worth $502                               | fixed                             | Money RECEIVED divided by orders PLACED, under a label that reads as the size of an order                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| [324](issues/324-her-shop-told-shoppers-she-had-nothing-to-sell.md)                                               | major    | Her shop told shoppers she had nothing to sell                                                           | fixed                             | With api-rest unreachable, every listing rendered "could not ask" as "there is nothing here" — `0 products` over 16 published garments, while a product page on the same site correctly said it had failed to load                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| [325](issues/325-the-console-said-sixteen-products-were-unfindable-on-a-screen-listing-seven.md)                  | minor    | The console said sixteen of her products could not be found, on a screen listing seven                   | fixed                             | The notice's count is its own query that no catalog write invalidated, and the pane's Refresh refetched the rows without it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| [326](issues/326-she-added-her-instagram-where-the-screen-said-it-would-appear-in-her-footer.md)                  | major    | She added her Instagram where the screen said it would appear in her footer, and it did not              | fixed                             | The seed emptied the footer's three social slots and put nothing live in their place, so `site.social` was resolved on every render and no node ever asked for it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| [327](issues/327-eleven-buttons-in-the-console-did-nothing-at-all-and-said-nothing-either.md)                     | major    | Eleven buttons in the console did nothing at all, and said nothing either                                | fixed                             | `controller.open` returns null for an unregistered key without a word, so eleven controls across My Site, Stock and the empty-workspace screen were dead and looked exactly like working ones — five still naming a pane split up months ago, five plain typos, and the one button whose job was to escape a dead end                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| [328](issues/328-the-home-screen-has-no-address-so-the-bar-keeps-naming-the-pane-before-it.md)                    | minor    | Home has no address, so the bar goes on naming whatever she was looking at before                        | fixed                             | Both consoles read one route table and `/home` already belongs to sparx's Home, so focusing Piggles' Home leaves the previous pane's address in the bar and a copied link sends the wrong screen                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| [329](issues/329-she-wrote-a-size-guide-and-nothing-on-her-site-linked-to-it.md)                                  | major    | She wrote a size guide, and nothing on her site linked to it                                             | fixed                             | The pre-publish check proved every LINK points at a real page and never that every PAGE has a link, so two pages she wrote and published were reachable only by typing the address, on a maker whose product pages discuss fit at length                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| [330](issues/330-every-photograph-on-her-site-was-measured-as-weighing-nothing.md)                                | major    | Every photograph on her site was measured as weighing nothing                                            | fixed                             | A remote picture is registered by URL and never downloaded, so its `byte_size` is 0 — and the weight report added that up as a measurement instead of counting it unsized, so a site of hot-linked 1600px photographs measured as pure markup                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| [331](issues/331-the-customer-list-threw-on-the-view-it-opens-on.md)                                              | blocker  | The customer list threw on the view it opens on                                                          | fixed                             | [322]'s fix asked Prisma for NULLS LAST on every sort field; Prisma refuses that form on a non-nullable column, and the default sort is one — so `customerService.list` threw for every caller, and the unit test asserting the query shape against a MOCKED client went green the whole time                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| [332](issues/332-her-customer-card-counted-three-orders-and-listed-a-different-three.md)                          | major    | Her customer card counted three orders and listed a different three                                      | fixed                             | The figures leave a cancelled order out and the list beneath them did not, so a card reading “3 orders, $582.60” sat over three rows summing to $636.90 — having pushed off the only order she had actually been paid for                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| [333](issues/333-the-screen-offered-her-a-backup-code-and-gave-her-a-keyboard-that-cannot-type-one.md)            | major    | The screen offered her a backup code and gave her a keyboard that cannot type one                        | fixed                             | The two-step step invites a backup code, then labels the field “six-digit”, raises a digits-only keypad a `6XWyo-LajdX` cannot be typed on, and tells a failed backup code to “try the current one” when it never changes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| [334](issues/334-look-and-feel-sent-her-to-a-screen-with-no-colors-on-it.md)                                      | major    | Look & feel sent her to a screen with no colors on it                                                    | fixed                             | The empty state's one sentence said her site's colors come from Business details, which is name, address, contact, tax and defaults, with no color on it at all                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| [335](issues/335-her-customers-were-told-to-ring-a-sales-rep-she-does-not-have.md)                                | major    | Her customers were told to ring a sales rep she does not have                                            | fixed                             | Her customer's account offered **Bookings** with a Book an appointment button leading to “no services are bookable”, and a **B2B Account** panel saying “contact your sales representative” — at a one-woman studio with no services, no B2B accounts and no sales reps                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| [336](issues/336-skip-to-what-you-are-working-on-landed-her-above-it.md)                                          | major    | “Skip to what you are working on” landed her above it                                                    | fixed                             | The skip control jumped the rail and stopped at `<main>`, which still holds the pane tab strip — three stops per open pane, 158 focusable controls, about a hundred presses between her and the pane it had just opened                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| [337](issues/337-she-made-a-photo-the-main-one-and-her-product-page-kept-the-old-one.md)                          | major    | She made a photo the main one and her product page kept the old one                                      | fixed                             | The card select ordered `isPrimary desc, position asc` and the product-detail select, forty lines below in the same file, ordered by position alone — so **Make it the main photo** moved it in the console and left the shopper's page on the old picture                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| [338](issues/338-the-words-she-wrote-for-blind-shoppers-were-used-on-one-page-only.md)                            | major    | The words she wrote for blind shoppers were used on one page only                                        | fixed                             | The console promises a photo's description is read aloud to shoppers who cannot see it; the list select fetched the thumbnail's id and no `alt`, so the shop grid, the home rails and search all announced the product title that is already the heading beside them                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| [339](issues/339-the-post-she-wrote-started-128px-right-of-its-own-headline.md)                                   | minor    | The post she wrote started 128px right of its own headline                                               | fixed                             | `articleBody()` narrowed the BAND to `max-w-3xl` while the masthead, photograph and back-link stayed `max-w-5xl`; `mx-auto` turned the difference into a 128px step, so the writing began to the right of its own headline. Her blog page has no stored tree, so the factory fix reaches her live posts                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| [340](issues/340-she-grouped-her-clothes-into-collections-nobody-could-click-through-to.md)                       | major    | She grouped her clothes into collections nobody could click through to                                   | fixed (56 blueprints pending)     | Seven collections with banner photographs and descriptions, behind a `/collections` page nothing on the site links to. `reach.ts` was built for this and borrowed `BUILTIN_PATHS` — the addresses that EXIST — to answer whether anything REACHES one, exempting all three seeded browse indexes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| [341](issues/341-her-shop-had-filters-and-no-way-to-search-it.md)                                                 | major    | Her shop had filters and no way to search it                                                             | fixed                             | No search box on any browse surface — `/products`, `/shop`, collections, categories or the header — although every one already read `?q=` and retitled itself for it; `q` existed only as a hidden field carrying a term in from `/search`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| [342](issues/342-her-size-filter-ran-small-medium-large-extra-small.md)                                           | minor    | Her size filter ran Small, Medium, Large, Extra Small                                                    | fixed                             | She declared XS/S/M/L/XL with positions and the panel rendered Typesense's count order, which ties when every garment comes in every size. A public `option-axes` read carries her declared order to all four browse surfaces; the merge across products is topological, because a position only means something inside the product that set it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| [343](issues/343-her-shop-served-another-companys-brand-color.md)                                                 | major    | Her shop served another company's brand color                                                            | fixed                             | The platform's last-resort site theme was one product's flagship look, and a failed frame fetch returned the same `null` as "no theme published" — so a blip painted another company's brand primary onto a live shop. The base is silicaui's own neutral now, and a failed read is retried and logged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| [344](issues/344-the-only-place-her-site-says-where-to-visit-was-its-smallest-sentence.md)                        | minor    | The only place her site says where to visit was its smallest sentence                                    | fixed                             | `enquiryForm()` ships the alternative-contact slot at `text-sm` because its sample copy is a phone number; she rewrote hers to her studio address and hours. Seven more terms and instructions across the kit were the same shape — delivery cost, what a price includes, "call ahead", an allergy instruction                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| [345](issues/345-the-biggest-photo-on-her-product-page-was-the-last-one-requested.md)                             | minor    | The biggest photo on her product page was the last one requested                                         | fixed                             | The LCP hero on every product page and journal post carried silicaui's `loading="lazy"` default, and the atom ignores a `loading` prop — so both heroes are raw `img` now. Cards stay lazy. 12 of 13 stored product trees earned a heal, recognized by the radius only the hero has                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| [346](issues/346-her-own-studio-called-her-business-something-else.md)                                            | minor    | Her own studio called her business something else                                                        | fixed                             | The canvas header and footer announced her shop as **Built for the work** for a second on every page open — `SAMPLE_TITLES[0]`, because `site.identity.name` is a text field whose key reads as a title, so the filler gave the business a sample HEADLINE in the two places that say whose site this is                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| [347](issues/347-adding-anything-to-a-page-meant-scrolling-past-118-rows-of-developer-words.md)                   | major    | Adding anything to a page meant scrolling past 118 rows of developer words                               | fixed                             | **118 rows** and six and a half screens of Container, Stack, Join and App Shell before the first group written in her language; the shelf's own ready-made sections sat at the bottom of a 12,816px list because `mergeCatalog` appends the host's groups after silicaui's                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| [348](issues/348-the-control-that-sets-a-pages-width-would-not-say-what-the-width-was.md)                         | major    | The control that sets a page's width would not say what the width was                                    | fixed                             | `state.value === option.value && !state.inherited` marked nothing at all when a value was inherited, so every chip row in the Design panel — width, spacing, type, color — showed six identical buttons; her About page was the site's only `max-w-2xl` and the one control that knew it would not say so                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| [349](issues/349-the-canvas-drew-pictures-the-live-page-would-not-and-skipped-ones-it-would.md)                   | major    | The canvas drew pictures the live page would not, and skipped ones it would                              | fixed                             | The canvas drew the shelf's placeholder `src=""` as a real empty `src` (a full re-fetch of the dev page per picture, six on a photo grid) and returned before `boundAttr` on every void tag, so bound product photographs never resolved in the editor. Both rules now run in the publish path's order                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| [350](issues/350-every-message-a-customer-sent-her-was-thrown-away-and-the-page-thanked-them.md)                  | blocker  | Every message a customer sent her was thrown away, and the page thanked them for it                      | fixed                             | Her Contact form sent NOTHING — zero fetches from a clean load — and settled to `success` anyway. `convert.ts`'s shared `form()` helper shipped the host ref `'submit'`, which the storefront routes nowhere, and an unrecognised ref is not an error at any layer: the handler returns, the promise resolves, the behavior thanks the visitor. All four shelf forms, every tenant site, since the file was written; the file's own header asserted the opposite                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| [351](issues/351-she-pressed-send-and-her-own-words-were-still-sitting-in-the-boxes.md)                           | major    | She pressed Send and her own words were still sitting in the boxes                                       | fixed                             | Separate from [350]: the visitor could not have known either way. The behavior's default status part is a 1x1px `clip-path: inset(50%)` live region, so the answer sat in the DOM one pixel wide and the boxes stayed full. `commerce.ts` had already hit this on the buy box and written it up; the shelf forms never got the same treatment. Now a real status line plus a specific success sentence per form                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| [352](issues/352-the-repair-shop-for-stale-pages-had-no-door-and-three-repairs-inside-it.md)                      | major    | The repair shop for stale pages had no door, and three repairs were sitting inside it                    | fixed                             | `upgradePageBody` was exported and imported by nothing. Its 45-line header describes a studio-load contract no code implemented, so the `gap-1.5` card repair, [345]'s lazy hero and [350]'s dead form ref were all inert on every tenant. `upgrade-frame.ts` had the identical hole until issue 296 and the page half was never done. Wiring only `load()` reproduced it — the studio opens ONE page through `loadPage`, caught by looking at the database after a real reload                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| [353](issues/353-the-column-telling-her-which-form-a-message-came-from-said-the-same-thing-on-every-row.md)       | minor    | The column telling her which form a message came from said the same thing on every row                   | fixed                             | Form replies' **Form** column read “Untitled form” on every row of every site and always would: the name comes from `FormDefinition.config.name` and NO console surface sets it (0 of 4 stored submissions carry one). The page was on 4 of 4 rows, rendered one line lower in the detail by a helper sitting directly beneath the one that ignored it, and the platform contract already said “empty ⇒ the inbox falls back to the page”                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| [354](issues/354-nothing-ticked-so-every-automation-stopped-the-moment-it-started.md)                             | major    | Nothing ticked, so every automation stopped the moment it started                                        | fixed                             | Consuming an event ENQUEUES a run; a TICK advances it, and the only thing that produced a tick was `k8s/cronjobs/automation-tick.yaml`. Outside the cluster nothing knocked on that door — the CronJob's own header predicts the symptom word for word. The first manual tick found **100 due runs and 20 due schedules** banked up, all completing cleanly. `event-worker` now drives its own minute heartbeat, safe beside the CronJob because the tick takes an advisory lock                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| [355](issues/355-she-could-not-say-who-should-be-told-when-somebody-writes-to-her.md)                             | major    | She could not say who should be told when somebody writes to her                                         | fixed                             | Name, notify, recipients, autoresponder and the CRM actions were all stored, validated, editor-gated and honoured by the submit path — and no console surface opened any of it, so every form on every site was unnamed and emailed the account fallback. Nothing was needed from silicaui: it contributes a `<form>` and an action ref, and stops. The picker also read `FormDefinition` ROWS, which exist only after a save, so it offered exactly the forms already configured — nothing.
